                                                            real(8) function code(x1, x2)
use fmin_fmax_functions
    real(8), intent (in) :: x1
    real(8), intent (in) :: x2
    real(8) :: t_0
    real(8) :: tmp
    t_0 = x1 * ((9.0d0 * x1) - 1.0d0)
    if (x1 <= (-2d-95)) then
        tmp = t_0
    else if (x1 <= 4d-125) then
        tmp = 1.0d0 / ((-0.16666666666666666d0) / x2)
